== example1
src title Go-Back-N: window 4, frame 2 lost\nlanes Sender Receiver\nseq SW 0..7 window=4 at=Sender\nvar expected = 0\n\nstep Send frames 0-3 without waiting\n  send Sender -> Receiver "0"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2" lost\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\nstep Receiver accepts 0 and 1; 3 is out of order and discarded\n  deliver Receiver "0"\n  deliver Receiver "1"\n  set expected = 2\n  discard Receiver "3"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 0"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\nstep ACK 1 arrives: window slides to 2-5, send 4 and 5\n  slide SW to=2\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"\nstep Timer for frame 2 expires: go back and resend 2-5\n  timer Sender "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2..5] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"

== example2
src title Bubble sort, first pass\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2]\nvar i = 0\nstep Compare A[0] and A[1]: 5 > 1, swap\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep Compare A[1] and A[2]: 5 > 4, swap\n  unmark A[0]\n  set i = 1\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep Compare A[2] and A[3]: 5 > 2, swap\n  unmark A[1]\n  set i = 2\n  swap A[2] A[3]\nstep 5 is in its final place\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3] done\n  say "After one pass the largest value has bubbled to the end."
exp title Bubble sort, first pass\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2]\nvar i = 0\nstep Compare A[0] and A[1]: 5 > 1, swap\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep Compare A[1] and A[2]: 5 > 4, swap\n  unmark A[0]\n  set i = 1\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep Compare A[2] and A[3]: 5 > 2, swap\n  unmark A[1]\n  set i = 2\n  swap A[2] A[3]\nstep 5 is in its final place\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3] done\n  say "After one pass the largest value has bubbled to the end."

== example4
src title Stop-and-wait vs selective repeat\nscene Stop-and-wait\n  lanes A B\n  step Send frame 0 and wait\n    send A -> B "0" lost\n    timer A "T0" start\n  step Timeout: resend 0\n    timer A "T0" expire\n    send A -> B "0"\n    deliver B "0"\nscene Selective repeat\n  lanes A B\n  seq W 0..5 window=3 at=A\n  step Send 0, 1, 2\n    send A -> B "0" lost\n    send A -> B "1"\n    send A -> B "2"\n    timer A "T0" start\n  step B buffers 1 and 2; only 0 is resent after its timer expires\n    buffer B "1"\n    buffer B "2"\n    timer A "T0" expire\n    send A -> B "0"\n    deliver B "0 1 2"\n    slide W to=3
exp title Stop-and-wait vs selective repeat\nscene Stop-and-wait\n  lanes A B\n  step Send frame 0 and wait\n    send A -> B "0" lost\n    timer A "T0" start\n  step Timeout: resend 0\n    timer A "T0" expire\n    send A -> B "0"\n    deliver B "0"\nscene Selective repeat\n  lanes A B\n  seq W 0..5 window=3 at=A\n  step Send 0, 1, 2\n    send A -> B "0" lost\n    send A -> B "1"\n    send A -> B "2"\n    timer A "T0" start\n  step B buffers 1 and 2; only 0 is resent after its timer expires\n    buffer B "1"\n    buffer B "2"\n    timer A "T0" expire\n    send A -> B "0"\n    deliver B "0 1 2"\n    slide W to=3

src title Reverse a linked list\nlist L = 1 -> 2 -> 3\npointer prev\npointer cur -> L[0]\nstep 1 now points back to prev (null); move both pointers forward\n  link L[0] -> null\n  move prev -> L[0]\n  move cur -> L[1]\nstep 2 points back to 1\n  link L[1] -> L[0]\n  move prev -> L[1]\n  move cur -> L[2]\nstep 3 points back to 2; prev is the new head\n  link L[2] -> L[1]\n  move prev -> L[2]\n  move cur -> null\n  mark L[2] done

src title Breadth-first search from A\ngraph G = A-B, A-C, B-D, C-D, D-E\nqueue Q = [A]\nstep Visit A, put its neighbors B and C in the queue\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[A] done\n  set G[A] = "d=0"\n  mark G[A-B] active\n  mark G[A-C] active\n  enqueue Q B\n  enqueue Q C\nstep Visit B: D is new\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[B] done\n  set G[B] = "d=1"\n  mark G[B-D] active\n  enqueue Q D\nstep Visit C: D is already queued\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[C] done\n  set G[C] = "d=1"\n  mark G[C-D] muted

== gbn-zh
src kit gbn frames=6 window=4 lose=2 lang=zh
exp title 回退 N 步（窗口 4）\nlanes 发送方 接收方\nseq SW 0..5 window=4 at=发送方\nvar 期望 = 0\nstep 发出 0~3\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "0"\n  timer 发送方 "T0" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2" lost\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "3"\nstep 接收方：交付 0、1；3 失序，丢弃；回 ACK 0、ACK 1、ACK 1（重复确认）\n  deliver 接收方 "0"\n  deliver 接收方 "1"\n  discard 接收方 "3"\n  set 期望 = 2\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 0"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 0、ACK 1、ACK 1；窗口滑到 2~5；发出 4、5\n  mark SW[0..1] done\n  timer 发送方 "T0" stop\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\n  slide SW to=2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "4"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "5"\nstep 接收方：4、5 失序，丢弃；回 ACK 1、ACK 1（重复确认）\n  discard 接收方 "4"\n  discard 接收方 "5"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 1、ACK 1；帧 2 超时：从 2 起全部重发\n  timer 发送方 "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2..5] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "3"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "4"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "5"\nstep 接收方：交付 2~5；回 ACK 2、ACK 3、ACK 4、ACK 5\n  deliver 接收方 "2"\n  deliver 接收方 "3"\n  deliver 接收方 "4"\n  deliver 接收方 "5"\n  set 期望 = 6\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 3"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 4"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 5"\nstep 收到 ACK 2、ACK 3、ACK 4、ACK 5；全部帧已确认\n  mark SW[2..5] done\n  timer 发送方 "T2" stop
title 回退 N 步（窗口 4）
steps 8 scenes 1

exp title 停等协议\nlanes 发送方 接收方\nseq SW 0..2 window=1 at=发送方\nvar 期望 = 0\nstep 发出 0\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "0"\n  timer 发送方 "T0" start\nstep 接收方：交付 0；回 ACK 0\n  deliver 接收方 "0"\n  set 期望 = 1\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 0"\nstep 收到 ACK 0；窗口滑到 1；发出 1\n  mark SW[0..0] done\n  timer 发送方 "T0" stop\n  slide SW to=1\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1" lost\n  timer 发送方 "T1" start\nstep 帧 1 超时，只重发它\n  timer 发送方 "T1" expire\n  mark SW[1] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1"\n  timer 发送方 "T1" start\nstep 接收方：交付 1；回 ACK 1\n  deliver 接收方 "1"\n  set 期望 = 2\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 1；窗口滑到 2；发出 2\n  mark SW[1..1] done\n  timer 发送方 "T1" stop\n  slide SW to=2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\nstep 接收方：交付 2；回 ACK 2\n  deliver 接收方 "2"\n  set 期望 = 3\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2" lost\nstep 帧 2 超时，只重发它\n  timer 发送方 "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\nstep 接收方：2 是重复帧；回 ACK 2\n  discard 接收方 "2"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2"\nstep 收到 ACK 2；全部帧已确认\n  mark SW[2..2] done\n  timer 发送方 "T2" stop

exp title 冒泡排序\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2, 3]\npointer j -> A[0]\nstep 第 1 趟：5 > 1，交换\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep 第 1 趟：5 > 4，交换\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep 第 1 趟：5 > 2，交换\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2..3] active\n  move j -> A[2]\n  swap A[2] A[3]\nstep 第 1 趟：5 > 3，交换；5 到达最终位置\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3..4] active\n  move j -> A[3]\n  swap A[3] A[4]\n  unmark A[3]\n  mark A[4] done\nstep 第 2 趟：1 ≤ 4，不动\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 第 2 趟：4 > 2，交换\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep 第 2 趟：4 > 3，交换；4 到达最终位置\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2..3] active\n  move j -> A[2]\n  swap A[2] A[3]\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3] done\nstep 第 3 趟：1 ≤ 2，不动\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 第 3 趟：2 ≤ 3，不动；3 到达最终位置\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2] done\nstep 这一趟没有交换，已经有序\n  mark A done\n  move j -> null

== insertion-zh
src kit insertion A=[b, d, a, c] lang=zh
exp title 直接插入排序\narray A = [b, d, a, c]\npointer key\nstep 取出 A[1] = d，往前找位置\n  move key -> A[1]\n  mark A[1] active\nstep b ≤ d，d 插在这里；前 2 个有序\n  unmark A[1]\nstep 取出 A[2] = a，往前找位置\n  move key -> A[2]\n  mark A[2] active\nstep d > a，d 后移\n  swap A[1] A[2]\n  move key -> A[1]\nstep b > a，b 后移\n  swap A[0] A[1]\n  move key -> A[0]\nstep 到头了，a 放在最前；前 3 个有序\n  unmark A[0]\nstep 取出 A[3] = c，往前找位置\n  move key -> A[3]\n  mark A[3] active\nstep d > c，d 后移\n  swap A[2] A[3]\n  move key -> A[2]\nstep b ≤ c，c 插在这里；前 4 个有序\n  unmark A[2]\nstep 排序完成\n  mark A done\n  move key -> null

exp title 快速排序（枢轴取末尾）\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2, 8, 3]\npointer i\npointer j\nstep 划分 A[0..5]，枢轴 = 3\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 5 ≥ 3：跳过\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 1 < 3：i 前进，交换 A[0] 和 A[1]\n  move j -> A[1]\n  move i -> A[0]\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep 4 ≥ 3：跳过\n  move j -> A[2]\nstep 2 < 3：i 前进，交换 A[1] 和 A[3]\n  move j -> A[3]\n  move i -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[3]\nstep 8 ≥ 3：跳过\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 枢轴 3 放到 A[2]，左边都比它小、右边都不小\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[2] A[5]\n  mark A[2] done\nstep 划分 A[0..1]，枢轴 = 2\n  mark A[1] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 1 < 2：i 前进\n  move j -> A[0]\n  move i -> A[0]\nstep 枢轴 2 放到 A[1]，左边都比它小、右边都不小\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  mark A[1] done\nstep A[0] 只有一个元素，已就位\n  mark A[0] done\nstep 划分 A[3..5]，枢轴 = 4\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[3]\nstep 5 ≥ 4：跳过\n  move j -> A[3]\nstep 8 ≥ 4：跳过\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 枢轴 4 放到 A[3]，左边都比它小、右边都不小\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[3] A[5]\n  mark A[3] done\nstep 划分 A[4..5]，枢轴 = 5\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 8 ≥ 5：跳过\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 枢轴 5 放到 A[4]，左边都比它小、右边都不小\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[4] A[5]\n  mark A[4] done\nstep A[5] 只有一个元素，已就位\n  mark A[5] done\nstep 排序完成\n  mark A done

== crc-zh
src kit crc data=101001 gen=1101 lang=zh
exp title CRC 循环冗余检验：101001 / 1101\nsheet 竖式\nwrite 竖式[1,0] "1101"\nwrite 竖式[1,4] ")"\nwrite 竖式[1,5] "101001000"\nrule 竖式[1,5..13] above\nmark 竖式[1,11..13] new\nlabel 竖式[0] "← 商 Q"\nlabel 竖式[1] "← 被除数：M 后补 3 个 0"\ntext "除数 1101 写在左边，从被除数最高位起，每次对齐除数做一次模 2 减（异或）"\nstep G = 1101 对应多项式 x³ + x² + 1，最高次幂是 3，所以 r = 3：校验码 R 有 3 位（= G 的位数 4 − 1）\n  mark 竖式[1,0..3] active\n  say "从左到右每一位对应 x 的 3 次、2 次……0 次，是 1 的位就有这一项"\nstep 在 M = 101001 后补 r = 3 个 0（相当于 M × 2³），得到被除数 101001000\n  unmark 竖式[1,0..3]\n  mark 竖式[1,11..13] active\n  say "补出来的 3 位留给余数：最后把余数填进这 3 位，整个帧就能被 G 整除"\nstep 第 1 次：取 4 位 1010，首位是 1，够除：商上 1，减（异或）1101，得 0111；落下被除数的下一位 0，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[1,11..13]\n  mark 竖式[1,5..8] active\n  write 竖式[0,8] "1"\n  write 竖式[2,5] "1101"\n  rule 竖式[2,5..8]\n  write 竖式[3,6] "111"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：1⊕1=0  0⊕1=1  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..3,9]\n  write 竖式[3,9] "0"\nstep 第 2 次：取 4 位 1110，首位是 1，够除：商上 1，减（异或）1101，得 0011；落下被除数的下一位 1，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[1,5..8]\n  mark 竖式[3,6..9] active\n  write 竖式[0,9] "1"\n  write 竖式[4,6] "1101"\n  rule 竖式[4,6..9]\n  write 竖式[5,7] "011"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..5,10]\n  write 竖式[5,10] "1"\nstep 第 3 次：取 4 位 0111，首位是 0，不够除：商上 0，减（异或）0000，不变；落下被除数的下一位 0，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[3,6..9]\n  mark 竖式[5,7..10] active\n  write 竖式[0,10] "0"\n  write 竖式[6,7] "0000"\n  rule 竖式[6,7..10]\n  write 竖式[7,8] "111"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：0⊕0=0  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..7,11]\n  write 竖式[7,11] "0"\nstep 第 4 次：取 4 位 1110，首位是 1，够除：商上 1，减（异或）1101，得 0011；落下被除数的下一位 0，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[5,7..10]\n  mark 竖式[7,8..11] active\n  write 竖式[0,11] "1"\n  write 竖式[8,8] "1101"\n  rule 竖式[8,8..11]\n  write 竖式[9,9] "011"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..9,12]\n  write 竖式[9,12] "0"\nstep 第 5 次：取 4 位 0110，首位是 0，不够除：商上 0，减（异或）0000，不变；落下被除数的下一位 0，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[7,8..11]\n  mark 竖式[9,9..12] active\n  write 竖式[0,12] "0"\n  write 竖式[10,9] "0000"\n  rule 竖式[10,9..12]\n  write 竖式[11,10] "110"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：0⊕0=0  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1  0⊕0=0。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..11,13]\n  write 竖式[11,13] "0"\nstep 第 6 次：取 4 位 1100，首位是 1，够除：商上 1，减（异或）1101，得 0001\n  unmark 竖式[9,9..12]\n  mark 竖式[11,10..13] active\n  write 竖式[0,13] "1"\n  write 竖式[12,10] "1101"\n  rule 竖式[12,10..13]\n  write 竖式[13,11] "001"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  0⊕0=0  0⊕1=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\nstep 被除数的每一位都用完了，最后剩下的 3 位就是余数 R = 001（不足 3 位也要补齐）；把它接在 M 后面，发送 101001001\n  unmark 竖式[11,10..13]\n  mark 竖式[13,11..13] done\n  label 竖式[13] "← 余数 R，作为 FCS"\n  say "接收方用收到的整个帧除以 1101，余数为 0 就认为没有出错"
title CRC 循环冗余检验：101001 / 1101

== dfs-zh

exp title 深度优先搜索（从 A 出发）\ngraph G = A->B, A->C, B->D, C->D, D->E\nqueue 访问序列 = []\nstack S = []\nstep 访问 A\n  push S A\n  mark G[A] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 A\nstep 访问 B\n  mark G[A->B] active\n  push S B\n  mark G[B] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 B\nstep 访问 D\n  mark G[B->D] active\n  push S D\n  mark G[D] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 D\nstep 访问 E\n  mark G[D->E] active\n  push S E\n  mark G[E] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 E\nstep E 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[E] done\nstep D 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[D] done\nstep B 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[B] done\nstep 访问 C\n  mark G[A->C] active\n  push S C\n  mark G[C] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 C\nstep C 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[C] done\nstep A 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[A] done\nstep 遍历结束：A B D E C

== dijkstra-zh

exp title Dijkstra 最短路径（从 A 出发）\ngraph G = A-B:4, A-C:2, C-B:1, B-D:5, C-D:8, E\nstep 起点 A 的距离为 0，其余为 ∞\n  set G[A] = "0"\n  set G[B] = "∞"\n  set G[C] = "∞"\n  set G[D] = "∞"\n  set G[E] = "∞"\n  mark G[A] active\nstep 未确定的点里 A 最近（0），确定它\n  mark G[A] done\nstep 经 A 更新：B：∞ → 4、C：∞ → 2\n  mark G[A-B] active\n  set G[B] = "4"\n  mark G[B] active\n  mark G[A-C] active\n  set G[C] = "2"\n  mark G[C] active\nstep 未确定的点里 C 最近（2），确定它\n  mark G[C] done\n  mark G[A-C] done\nstep 经 C 更新：B：4 → 3、D：∞ → 10\n  unmark G[A-B]\n  mark G[C-B] active\n  set G[B] = "3"\n  mark G[B] active\n  mark G[C-D] active\n  set G[D] = "10"\n  mark G[D] active\nstep 未确定的点里 B 最近（3），确定它\n  mark G[B] done\n  mark G[C-B] done\nstep 经 B 更新：D：10 → 8\n  unmark G[C-D]\n  mark G[B-D] active\n  set G[D] = "8"\n  mark G[D] active\nstep 未确定的点里 D 最近（8），确定它\n  mark G[D] done\n  mark G[B-D] done\nstep 全部确定。E 不可达

== gbn-ack-en

exp title Go-back-N (window 4)\nlanes Sender Receiver\nseq SW 0..5 window=4 at=Sender mod=8\nvar expected = 0\nstep send 0~3\n  send Sender -> Receiver "0"\n  timer Sender "T0" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2" lost\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3" lost\nstep Receiver: deliver 0, 1; reply ACK 0, ACK 1\n  deliver Receiver "0"\n  deliver Receiver "1"\n  set expected = 2\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 0"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\nstep got ACK 0, ACK 1; window slides to 2~5; send 4, 5\n  mark SW[0..1] done\n  timer Sender "T0" stop\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  slide SW to=2\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"\nstep Receiver: 4, 5 out of order, discarded; reply ACK 1, ACK 1 (duplicate ACKs)\n  discard Receiver "4"\n  discard Receiver "5"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\nstep frame 2 times out: resend everything from 2\n  timer Sender "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2..5] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"\nstep Receiver: deliver 2~5; reply ACK 2, ACK 3, ACK 4, ACK 5\n  deliver Receiver "2"\n  deliver Receiver "3"\n  deliver Receiver "4"\n  deliver Receiver "5"\n  set expected = 6\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 2"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 3"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 4" lost\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 5"\nstep got ACK 2, ACK 3, ACK 5; every frame is acknowledged\n  mark SW[2..5] done\n  timer Sender "T2" stop

== sr-en
src kit sr frames=8 window=4 lose=2,6 loseack=3 lang=en
exp title Selective repeat (window 4)\nlanes Sender Receiver\nseq SW 0..7 window=4 at=Sender\nseq RW 0..7 window=4 at=Receiver\nstep send 0~3\n  send Sender -> Receiver "0"\n  timer Sender "T0" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1"\n  timer Sender "T1" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2" lost\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\n  timer Sender "T3" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 0, 1; buffer 3; reply ACK 0, ACK 1, ACK 3\n  deliver Receiver "0"\n  unmark RW[0..0]\n  slide RW to=1\n  deliver Receiver "1"\n  unmark RW[1..1]\n  slide RW to=2\n  buffer Receiver "3"\n  mark RW[3] active\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 0"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 3" lost\nstep got ACK 0, ACK 1; window slides to 2~5; send 4, 5\n  timer Sender "T0" stop\n  timer Sender "T1" stop\n  mark SW[0] done\n  mark SW[1] done\n  slide SW to=2\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  timer Sender "T4" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"\n  timer Sender "T5" start\nstep Receiver: buffer 4, 5; reply ACK 4, ACK 5\n  buffer Receiver "4"\n  mark RW[4] active\n  buffer Receiver "5"\n  mark RW[5] active\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 4"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 5"\nstep got ACK 4, ACK 5; frame 2, 3 times out: resend only that\n  timer Sender "T4" stop\n  timer Sender "T5" stop\n  mark SW[4] done\n  mark SW[5] done\n  timer Sender "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2] active\n  timer Sender "T3" expire\n  mark SW[3] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\n  timer Sender "T3" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 2~5; 3 is a duplicate; reply ACK 2, ACK 3\n  deliver Receiver "2 3 4 5"\n  unmark RW[2..5]\n  slide RW to=6\n  note Receiver "dup 3" state=muted\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 2"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 3"\nstep got ACK 2, ACK 3; window slides to 6~7; send 6, 7\n  timer Sender "T2" stop\n  timer Sender "T3" stop\n  mark SW[2] done\n  mark SW[3] done\n  slide SW to=6\n  send Sender -> Receiver "6" lost\n  timer Sender "T6" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "7"\n  timer Sender "T7" start\nstep Receiver: buffer 7; reply ACK 7\n  buffer Receiver "7"\n  mark RW[7] active\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 7"\nstep got ACK 7\n  timer Sender "T7" stop\n  mark SW[7] done\nstep frame 6 times out: resend only that\n  timer Sender "T6" expire\n  mark SW[6] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "6"\n  timer Sender "T6" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 6, 7; reply ACK 6\n  deliver Receiver "6 7"\n  unmark RW[6..7]\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 6"\nstep got ACK 6; every frame is acknowledged\n  timer Sender "T6" stop\n  mark SW[6] done
title Selective repeat (window 4)
steps 13 scenes 1

exp title Stop-and-wait\nlanes Sender Receiver\nseq SW 0..2 window=1 at=Sender\nvar expected = 0\nstep send 0\n  send Sender -> Receiver "0"\n  timer Sender "T0" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 0; reply ACK 0\n  deliver Receiver "0"\n  set expected = 1\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 0"\nstep got ACK 0; window slides to 1; send 1\n  mark SW[0..0] done\n  timer Sender "T0" stop\n  slide SW to=1\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1" lost\n  timer Sender "T1" start\nstep frame 1 times out: resend only that\n  timer Sender "T1" expire\n  mark SW[1] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1"\n  timer Sender "T1" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 1; reply ACK 1\n  deliver Receiver "1"\n  set expected = 2\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\nstep got ACK 1; window slides to 2; send 2\n  mark SW[1..1] done\n  timer Sender "T1" stop\n  slide SW to=2\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  timer Sender "T2" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 2; reply ACK 2\n  deliver Receiver "2"\n  set expected = 3\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 2" lost\nstep frame 2 times out: resend only that\n  timer Sender "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  timer Sender "T2" start\nstep Receiver: 2 is a duplicate; reply ACK 2\n  discard Receiver "2"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 2"\nstep got ACK 2; every frame is acknowledged\n  mark SW[2..2] done\n  timer Sender "T2" stop

exp title Bubble sort\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2, 3]\npointer j -> A[0]\nstep Pass 1: 5 > 1: swap\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep Pass 1: 5 > 4: swap\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep Pass 1: 5 > 2: swap\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2..3] active\n  move j -> A[2]\n  swap A[2] A[3]\nstep Pass 1: 5 > 3: swap; 5 is in place\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3..4] active\n  move j -> A[3]\n  swap A[3] A[4]\n  unmark A[3]\n  mark A[4] done\nstep Pass 2: 1 ≤ 4: keep\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep Pass 2: 4 > 2: swap\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep Pass 2: 4 > 3: swap; 4 is in place\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2..3] active\n  move j -> A[2]\n  swap A[2] A[3]\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3] done\nstep Pass 3: 1 ≤ 2: keep\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep Pass 3: 2 ≤ 3: keep; 3 is in place\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2] done\nstep No swaps in this pass: sorted\n  mark A done\n  move j -> null

== selection-en

exp title Selection sort\narray A = [3, 1, 3, 2, 10]\npointer i -> A[0]\npointer j\npointer min -> A[0]\nstep Round 1: find the minimum from A[0]; min = 3 so far\n  move i -> A[0]\n  move min -> A[0]\n  mark A[0] active\nstep 1 < 3: it becomes min\n  move j -> A[1]\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1] active\n  move min -> A[1]\nstep 3 ≥ 1: min stays\n  move j -> A[2]\nstep 2 ≥ 1: min stays\n  move j -> A[3]\nstep 10 ≥ 1: min stays\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep swap A[0] and A[1]: 1 is in place\n  move j -> null\n  unmark A[1]\n  swap A[0] A[1]\n  mark A[0] done\nstep Round 2: find the minimum from A[1]; min = 3 so far\n  move i -> A[1]\n  move min -> A[1]\n  mark A[1] active\nstep 3 ≥ 3: min stays\n  move j -> A[2]\nstep 2 < 3: it becomes min\n  move j -> A[3]\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[3] active\n  move min -> A[3]\nstep 10 ≥ 2: min stays\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep swap A[1] and A[3]: 2 is in place\n  move j -> null\n  unmark A[3]\n  swap A[1] A[3]\n  mark A[1] done\nstep Round 3: find the minimum from A[2]; min = 3 so far\n  move i -> A[2]\n  move min -> A[2]\n  mark A[2] active\nstep 3 ≥ 3: min stays\n  move j -> A[3]\nstep 10 ≥ 3: min stays\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep the minimum is already at A[2]\n  move j -> null\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[2] done\nstep Round 4: find the minimum from A[3]; min = 3 so far\n  move i -> A[3]\n  move min -> A[3]\n  mark A[3] active\nstep 10 ≥ 3: min stays\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep the minimum is already at A[3]\n  move j -> null\n  unmark A[3]\n  mark A[3] done\nstep Sorted\n  mark A done\n  move i -> null\n  move min -> null

== insertion-en
src kit insertion A=[b, d, a, c] lang=en
exp title Insertion sort\narray A = [b, d, a, c]\npointer key\nstep take A[1] = d and look for its place\n  move key -> A[1]\n  mark A[1] active\nstep b ≤ d: d goes here; the first 2 are sorted\n  unmark A[1]\nstep take A[2] = a and look for its place\n  move key -> A[2]\n  mark A[2] active\nstep d > a: shift d right\n  swap A[1] A[2]\n  move key -> A[1]\nstep b > a: shift b right\n  swap A[0] A[1]\n  move key -> A[0]\nstep reached the front: a goes first; the first 3 are sorted\n  unmark A[0]\nstep take A[3] = c and look for its place\n  move key -> A[3]\n  mark A[3] active\nstep d > c: shift d right\n  swap A[2] A[3]\n  move key -> A[2]\nstep b ≤ c: c goes here; the first 4 are sorted\n  unmark A[2]\nstep Sorted\n  mark A done\n  move key -> null
title Insertion sort
steps 11 scenes 1
tl 0 0.000
st 0 0 cap=\ncells:A=b:normal,d:normal,a:normal,c:normal\nptr:key=null\ncursor=0.000
st 0 1 cap=take A[1] = d and look for its place\ncells:A=b:normal,d:active,a:normal,c:normal\nptr:key=idx(A,1)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 2 cap=b ≤ d: d goes here; the first 2 are sorted\ncells:A=b:normal,d:normal,a:normal,c:normal\nptr:key=idx(A,1)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 3 cap=take A[2] = a and look for its place\ncells:A=b:normal,d:normal,a:active,c:normal\nptr:key=idx(A,2)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 4 cap=d > a: shift d right\ncells:A=b:normal,a:active,d:normal,c:normal\nptr:key=idx(A,1)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 5 cap=b > a: shift b right\ncells:A=a:active,b:normal,d:normal,c:normal\nptr:key=idx(A,0)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 6 cap=reached the front: a goes first; the first 3 are sorted\ncells:A=a:normal,b:normal,d:normal,c:normal\nptr:key=idx(A,0)\ncursor=0.000

== quick-en
src kit quick A=[5, 1, 4, 2, 8, 3] lang=en
exp title Quicksort (last element as pivot)\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2, 8, 3]\npointer i\npointer j\nstep partition A[0..5] around pivot 3\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 5 ≥ 3: skip\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 1 < 3: advance i, swap A[0] and A[1]\n  move j -> A[1]\n  move i -> A[0]\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep 4 ≥ 3: skip\n  move j -> A[2]\nstep 2 < 3: advance i, swap A[1] and A[3]\n  move j -> A[3]\n  move i -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[3]\nstep 8 ≥ 3: skip\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep pivot 3 goes to A[2]: smaller on the left, the rest on the right\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[2] A[5]\n  mark A[2] done\nstep partition A[0..1] around pivot 2\n  mark A[1] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 1 < 2: advance i\n  move j -> A[0]\n  move i -> A[0]\nstep pivot 2 goes to A[1]: smaller on the left, the rest on the right\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  mark A[1] done\nstep A[0] alone: in place\n  mark A[0] done\nstep partition A[3..5] around pivot 4\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[3]\nstep 5 ≥ 4: skip\n  move j -> A[3]\nstep 8 ≥ 4: skip\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep pivot 4 goes to A[3]: smaller on the left, the rest on the right\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[3] A[5]\n  mark A[3] done\nstep partition A[4..5] around pivot 5\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 8 ≥ 5: skip\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep pivot 5 goes to A[4]: smaller on the left, the rest on the right\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[4] A[5]\n  mark A[4] done\nstep A[5] alone: in place\n  mark A[5] done\nstep Sorted\n  mark A done

== crc-en
src kit crc data=101001 gen=1101 lang=en
exp title CRC：101001 / 1101\nsheet work\nwrite work[1,0] "1101"\nwrite work[1,4] ")"\nwrite work[1,5] "101001000"\nrule work[1,5..13] above\nmark work[1,11..13] new\nlabel work[0] "← quotient Q"\nlabel work[1] "← dividend: M followed by 3 zeros"\ntext "Divisor 1101 on the left; from the leftmost bit, align it and subtract modulo 2 (XOR) each time"\nstep G = 1101 is the polynomial x³ + x² + 1; its highest power is 3, so r = 3: the check bits R are 3 bits (= 4 bits of G − 1)\n  mark work[1,0..3] active\n  say "From left to right the bits stand for the powers 3, 2, …, 0 of x; a 1 means that term is present"\nstep append r = 3 zeros to M = 101001 (that is M × 2³): the dividend is 101001000\n  unmark work[1,0..3]\n  mark work[1,11..13] active\n  say "The 3 new bits are room for the remainder: filling them with it makes the whole frame divisible by G"\nstep Step 1: take 4 bits 1010: leading 1, so G fits: quotient bit 1, subtract (XOR) 1101 → 0111; bring down the next dividend bit 0 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[1,11..13]\n  mark work[1,5..8] active\n  write work[0,8] "1"\n  write work[2,5] "1101"\n  rule work[2,5..8]\n  write work[3,6] "111"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 1⊕1=0  0⊕1=1  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..3,9]\n  write work[3,9] "0"\nstep Step 2: take 4 bits 1110: leading 1, so G fits: quotient bit 1, subtract (XOR) 1101 → 0011; bring down the next dividend bit 1 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[1,5..8]\n  mark work[3,6..9] active\n  write work[0,9] "1"\n  write work[4,6] "1101"\n  rule work[4,6..9]\n  write work[5,7] "011"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..5,10]\n  write work[5,10] "1"\nstep Step 3: take 4 bits 0111: leading 0, so G does not fit: quotient bit 0, XOR 0000 changes nothing; bring down the next dividend bit 0 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[3,6..9]\n  mark work[5,7..10] active\n  write work[0,10] "0"\n  write work[6,7] "0000"\n  rule work[6,7..10]\n  write work[7,8] "111"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 0⊕0=0  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..7,11]\n  write work[7,11] "0"\nstep Step 4: take 4 bits 1110: leading 1, so G fits: quotient bit 1, subtract (XOR) 1101 → 0011; bring down the next dividend bit 0 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[5,7..10]\n  mark work[7,8..11] active\n  write work[0,11] "1"\n  write work[8,8] "1101"\n  rule work[8,8..11]\n  write work[9,9] "011"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..9,12]\n  write work[9,12] "0"\nstep Step 5: take 4 bits 0110: leading 0, so G does not fit: quotient bit 0, XOR 0000 changes nothing; bring down the next dividend bit 0 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[7,8..11]\n  mark work[9,9..12] active\n  write work[0,12] "0"\n  write work[10,9] "0000"\n  rule work[10,9..12]\n  write work[11,10] "110"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 0⊕0=0  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1  0⊕0=0. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..11,13]\n  write work[11,13] "0"\nstep Step 6: take 4 bits 1100: leading 1, so G fits: quotient bit 1, subtract (XOR) 1101 → 0001\n  unmark work[9,9..12]\n  mark work[11,10..13] active\n  write work[0,13] "1"\n  write work[12,10] "1101"\n  rule work[12,10..13]\n  write work[13,11] "001"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  0⊕0=0  0⊕1=1. exp title Level-order traversal\ntree T = (1 (2 4 5) (3 _ 7))\nqueue output = []\npointer p\nstep level order: level by level, left to right; visit 1\n  move p -> T[1]\n  mark T[1] active\n  enqueue output 1\nstep visit 2\n  mark T[1] done\n  move p -> T[2]\n  mark T[2] active\n  enqueue output 2\nstep visit 3\n  mark T[2] done\n  move p -> T[3]\n  mark T[3] active\n  enqueue output 3\nstep visit 4\n  mark T[3] done\n  move p -> T[4]\n  mark T[4] active\n  enqueue output 4\nstep visit 5\n  mark T[4] done\n  move p -> T[5]\n  mark T[5] active\n  enqueue output 5\nstep visit 7\n  mark T[5] done\n  move p -> T[7]\n  mark T[7] active\n  enqueue output 7\nstep Done: 1 2 3 4 5 7\n  mark T[7] done\n  move p -> null
title Level-order traversal
steps 8 scenes 1
tl 0 0.000
st 0 0 cap=\ncells:output=\ntree:T=1:normal(2:normal(4:normal() 5:normal()) 3:normal(_ 7:normal()))\nptr:p=null\ncursor=0.000
st 0 1 cap=level order: level by level, left to right; visit 1\ncells:output=1:new\ntree:T=1:active(2:normal(4:normal() 5:normal()) 3:normal(_ 7:normal()))\nptr:p=tnode(T,1)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 2 cap=visit 2\ncells:output=1:normal,2:new\ntree:T=1:done(2:active(4:normal() 5:normal()) 3:normal(_ 7:normal()))\nptr:p=tnode(T,2)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 3 cap=visit 3\ncells:output=1:normal,2:normal,3:new\ntree:T=1:done(2:done(4:normal() 5:normal()) 3:active(_ 7:normal()))\nptr:p=tnode(T,3)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 4 cap=visit 4\ncells:output=1:normal,2:normal,3:normal,4:new\ntree:T=1:done(2:done(4:active() 5:normal()) 3:done(_ 7:normal()))\nptr:p=tnode(T,4)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 5 cap=visit 5\ncells:output=1:normal,2:normal,3:normal,4:normal,5:new\ntree:T=1:done(2:done(4:done() 5:active()) 3:done(_ 7:normal()))\nptr:p=tnode(T,5)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 6 cap=visit 7\ncells:output=1:normal,2:normal,3:normal,4:normal,5:normal,7:new\ntree:T=1:done(2:done(4:done() 5:done()) 3:done(_ 7:active()))\nptr:p=tnode(T,7)\ncursor=0.000
st 0 7 cap=Done: 1 2 3 4 5 7\ncells:output=1:normal,2:normal,3:normal,4:normal,5:normal,7:normal\ntree:T=1:done(2:done(4:done() 5:done()) 3:done(_ 7:done()))\nptr:p=null\ncursor=0.000
== bfs-en
src kit bfs G="A-B, A-C, B-D, C-D, D-E" lang=en
exp title Breadth-first search from A\ngraph G = A-B, A-C, B-D, C-D, D-E\nqueue order = []\nqueue Q = []\nstep start at A: enqueue it\n  mark G[A] active\n  enqueue Q A\nstep dequeue and visit A; enqueue neighbors B, C\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[A] done\n  enqueue order A\n  mark G[A-B] active\n  mark G[B] active\n  enqueue Q B\n  mark G[A-C] active\n  mark G[C] active\n  enqueue Q C\nstep dequeue and visit B; enqueue neighbors D\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[B] done\n  enqueue order B\n  mark G[B-D] active\n  mark G[D] active\n  enqueue Q D\nstep dequeue and visit C; no new neighbors\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[C] done\n  enqueue order C\nstep dequeue and visit D; enqueue neighbors E\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[D] done\n  enqueue order D\n  mark G[D-E] active\n  mark G[E] active\n  enqueue Q E\nstep dequeue and visit E; no new neighbors\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[E] done\n  enqueue order E\nstep queue empty, done: A B C D E
title Breadth-first search from A
steps 8 scenes 1
tl 0 0.000
st 0 0 cap=\ncells:Q=\ncells:order=\ngraph:G=A:normal:;B:normal:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A-B::normal;A-C::normal;B-D::normal;C-D::normal;D-E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 1 cap=start at A: enqueue it\ncells:Q=A:new\ncells:order=\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:normal:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A-B::normal;A-C::normal;B-D::normal;C-D::normal;D-E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 2 cap=dequeue and visit A; enqueue neighbors B, C\ncells:Q=B:new,C:new\ncells:order=A:new\ngraph:G=A:done:;B:active:;C:active:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A-B::active;A-C::active;B-D::normal;C-D::normal;D-E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 3 cap=dequeue and visit B; enqueue neighbors D\ncells:Q=C:normal,D:new\ncells:order=A:normal,B:new\ngraph:G=A:done:;B:done:;C:active:;D:active:;E:normal:/A-B::active;A-C::active;B-D::active;C-D::normal;D-E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 4 cap=dequeue and visit C; no new neighbors\ncells:Q=D:normal\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,C:new\ngraph:G=A:done:;B:done:;C:done:;D:active:;E:normal:/A-B::active;A-C::active;B-D::active;C-D::normal;D-E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 5 cap=dequeue and visit D; enqueue neighbors E\ncells:Q=E:new\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,C:normal,D:new\ngraph:G=A:done:;B:done:;C:done:;D:done:;E:active:/A-B::active;A-C::active;B-D::active;C-D::normal;D-E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 6 cap=dequeue and visit E; no new neighbors\ncells:Q=\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,C:normal,D:normal,E:new\ngraph:G=A:done:;B:done:;C:done:;D:done:;E:done:/A-B::active;A-C::active;B-D::active;C-D::normal;D-E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 7 cap=queue empty, done: A B C D E\ncells:Q=\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,C:normal,D:normal,E:normal\ngraph:G=A:done:;B:done:;C:done:;D:done:;E:done:/A-B::active;A-C::active;B-D::active;C-D::normal;D-E::active\ncursor=0.000
== dfs-en
src kit dfs G="A->B, A->C, B->D, C->D, D->E" from=A lang=en
exp title Depth-first search from A\ngraph G = A->B, A->C, B->D, C->D, D->E\nqueue order = []\nstack S = []\nstep visit A\n  push S A\n  mark G[A] active\n  enqueue order A\nstep visit B\n  mark G[A->B] active\n  push S B\n  mark G[B] active\n  enqueue order B\nstep visit D\n  mark G[B->D] active\n  push S D\n  mark G[D] active\n  enqueue order D\nstep visit E\n  mark G[D->E] active\n  push S E\n  mark G[E] active\n  enqueue order E\nstep all neighbors of E visited: back up\n  pop S\n  mark G[E] done\nstep all neighbors of D visited: back up\n  pop S\n  mark G[D] done\nstep all neighbors of B visited: back up\n  pop S\n  mark G[B] done\nstep visit C\n  mark G[A->C] active\n  push S C\n  mark G[C] active\n  enqueue order C\nstep all neighbors of C visited: back up\n  pop S\n  mark G[C] done\nstep all neighbors of A visited: back up\n  pop S\n  mark G[A] done\nstep done: A B D E C
title Depth-first search from A
steps 12 scenes 1
tl 0 0.000
st 0 0 cap=\ncells:S=\ncells:order=\ngraph:G=A:normal:;B:normal:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A>B::normal;A>C::normal;B>D::normal;C>D::normal;D>E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 1 cap=visit A\ncells:S=A:new\ncells:order=A:new\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:normal:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A>B::normal;A>C::normal;B>D::normal;C>D::normal;D>E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 2 cap=visit B\ncells:S=A:normal,B:new\ncells:order=A:normal,B:new\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:active:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A>B::active;A>C::normal;B>D::normal;C>D::normal;D>E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 3 cap=visit D\ncells:S=A:normal,B:normal,D:new\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:new\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:active:;C:normal:;D:active:;E:normal:/A>B::active;A>C::normal;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 4 cap=visit E\ncells:S=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:new\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:new\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:active:;C:normal:;D:active:;E:active:/A>B::active;A>C::normal;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 5 cap=all neighbors of E visited: back up\ncells:S=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:normal\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:active:;C:normal:;D:active:;E:done:/A>B::active;A>C::normal;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 6 cap=all neighbors of D visited: back up\ncells:S=A:normal,B:normal\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:normal\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:active:;C:normal:;D:done:;E:done:/A>B::active;A>C::normal;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 7 cap=all neighbors of B visited: back up\ncells:S=A:normal\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:normal\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:done:;C:normal:;D:done:;E:done:/A>B::active;A>C::normal;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 8 cap=visit C\ncells:S=A:normal,C:new\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:normal,C:new\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:done:;C:active:;D:done:;E:done:/A>B::active;A>C::active;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 9 cap=all neighbors of C visited: back up\ncells:S=A:normal\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:normal,C:normal\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:done:;C:done:;D:done:;E:done:/A>B::active;A>C::active;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 10 cap=all neighbors of A visited: back up\ncells:S=\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:normal,C:normal\ngraph:G=A:done:;B:done:;C:done:;D:done:;E:done:/A>B::active;A>C::active;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 11 cap=done: A B D E C\ncells:S=\ncells:order=A:normal,B:normal,D:normal,E:normal,C:normal\ngraph:G=A:done:;B:done:;C:done:;D:done:;E:done:/A>B::active;A>C::active;B>D::active;C>D::normal;D>E::active\ncursor=0.000
== dijkstra-en
src kit dijkstra G="A-B:4, A-C:2, C-B:1, B-D:5, C-D:8, E" from=A lang=en
exp title Dijkstra shortest paths from A\ngraph G = A-B:4, A-C:2, C-B:1, B-D:5, C-D:8, E\nstep distance 0 at A, ∞ elsewhere\n  set G[A] = "0"\n  set G[B] = "∞"\n  set G[C] = "∞"\n  set G[D] = "∞"\n  set G[E] = "∞"\n  mark G[A] active\nstep A is the closest unfinished node (0): finalize it\n  mark G[A] done\nstep relax through A: B：∞ → 4, C：∞ → 2\n  mark G[A-B] active\n  set G[B] = "4"\n  mark G[B] active\n  mark G[A-C] active\n  set G[C] = "2"\n  mark G[C] active\nstep C is the closest unfinished node (2): finalize it\n  mark G[C] done\n  mark G[A-C] done\nstep relax through C: B：4 → 3, D：∞ → 10\n  unmark G[A-B]\n  mark G[C-B] active\n  set G[B] = "3"\n  mark G[B] active\n  mark G[C-D] active\n  set G[D] = "10"\n  mark G[D] active\nstep B is the closest unfinished node (3): finalize it\n  mark G[B] done\n  mark G[C-B] done\nstep relax through B: D：10 → 8\n  unmark G[C-D]\n  mark G[B-D] active\n  set G[D] = "8"\n  mark G[D] active\nstep D is the closest unfinished node (8): finalize it\n  mark G[D] done\n  mark G[B-D] done\nstep all finalized. unreachable: E
title Dijkstra shortest paths from A
steps 10 scenes 1
tl 0 0.000
st 0 0 cap=\ngraph:G=A:normal:;B:normal:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:normal;C-B:1:normal;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 1 cap=distance 0 at A, ∞ elsewhere\ngraph:G=A:active:0;B:normal:∞;C:normal:∞;D:normal:∞;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:normal;C-B:1:normal;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 2 cap=A is the closest unfinished node (0): finalize it\ngraph:G=A:done:0;B:normal:∞;C:normal:∞;D:normal:∞;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:normal;C-B:1:normal;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 3 cap=relax through A: B：∞ → 4, C：∞ → 2\ngraph:G=A:done:0;B:active:4;C:active:2;D:normal:∞;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:active;A-C:2:active;C-B:1:normal;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 4 cap=C is the closest unfinished node (2): finalize it\ngraph:G=A:done:0;B:active:4;C:done:2;D:normal:∞;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:active;A-C:2:done;C-B:1:normal;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 5 cap=relax through C: B：4 → 3, D：∞ → 10\ngraph:G=A:done:0;B:active:3;C:done:2;D:active:10;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:done;C-B:1:active;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 6 cap=B is the closest unfinished node (3): finalize it\ngraph:G=A:done:0;B:done:3;C:done:2;D:active:10;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:done;C-B:1:done;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:active\ncursor=0.000
st 0 7 cap=relax through B: D：10 → 8\ngraph:G=A:done:0;B:done:3;C:done:2;D:active:8;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:done;C-B:1:done;B-D:5:active;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 8 cap=D is the closest unfinished node (8): finalize it\ngraph:G=A:done:0;B:done:3;C:done:2;D:done:8;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:done;C-B:1:done;B-D:5:done;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 9 cap=all finalized. unreachable: E\ngraph:G=A:done:0;B:done:3;C:done:2;D:done:8;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:done;C-B:1:done;B-D:5:done;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
== compare-kits
src title 对比\nscene 停等\n  kit stopwait frames=3 lose=2 lang=zh\nscene 回退 N 步\n  kit gbn frames=6 window=3 lose=2 lang=zh\nscene 选择重传\n  kit sr frames=6 window=3 lose=2 lang=zh
exp title 对比\nscene 停等\nlanes 发送方 接收方\nseq SW 0..2 window=1 at=发送方\nvar 期望 = 0\nstep 发出 0\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "0"\n  timer 发送方 "T0" start\nstep 接收方：交付 0；回 ACK 0\n  deliver 接收方 "0"\n  set 期望 = 1\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 0"\nstep 收到 ACK 0；窗口滑到 1；发出 1\n  mark SW[0..0] done\n  timer 发送方 "T0" stop\n  slide SW to=1\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1"\n  timer 发送方 "T1" start\nstep 接收方：交付 1；回 ACK 1\n  deliver 接收方 "1"\n  set 期望 = 2\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 1；窗口滑到 2；发出 2\n  mark SW[1..1] done\n  timer 发送方 "T1" stop\n  slide SW to=2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2" lost\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\nstep 帧 2 超时，只重发它\n  timer 发送方 "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\nstep 接收方：交付 2；回 ACK 2\n  deliver 接收方 "2"\n  set 期望 = 3\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2"\nstep 收到 ACK 2；全部帧已确认\n  mark SW[2..2] done\n  timer 发送方 "T2" stop\nscene 回退 N 步\nlanes 发送方 接收方\nseq SW 0..5 window=3 at=发送方\nvar 期望 = 0\nstep 发出 0~2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "0"\n  timer 发送方 "T0" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2" lost\nstep 接收方：交付 0、1；回 ACK 0、ACK 1\n  deliver 接收方 "0"\n  deliver 接收方 "1"\n  set 期望 = 2\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 0"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 0、ACK 1；窗口滑到 2~4；发出 3、4\n  mark SW[0..1] done\n  timer 发送方 "T0" stop\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\n  slide SW to=2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "3"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "4"\nstep 接收方：3、4 失序，丢弃；回 ACK 1、ACK 1（重复确认）\n  discard 接收方 "3"\n  discard 接收方 "4"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 1、ACK 1；帧 2 超时：从 2 起全部重发\n  timer 发送方 "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2..4] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "3"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "4"\nstep 接收方：交付 2~4；回 ACK 2、ACK 3、ACK 4\n  deliver 接收方 "2"\n  deliver 接收方 "3"\n  deliver 接收方 "4"\n  set 期望 = 5\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 3"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 4"\nstep 收到 ACK 2、ACK 3、ACK 4；窗口滑到 5~5；发出 5\n  mark SW[2..4] done\n  timer 发送方 "T2" stop\n  slide SW to=5\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "5"\n  timer 发送方 "T5" start\nstep 接收方：交付 5；回 ACK 5\n  deliver 接收方 "5"\n  set 期望 = 6\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 5"\nstep 收到 ACK 5；全部帧已确认\n  mark SW[5..5] done\n  timer 发送方 "T5" stop\nscene 选择重传\nlanes 发送方 接收方\nseq SW 0..5 window=3 at=发送方\nseq RW 0..5 window=3 at=接收方\nstep 发出 0~2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "0"\n  timer 发送方 "T0" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1"\n  timer 发送方 "T1" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2" lost\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\nstep 接收方：交付 0、1；回 ACK 0、ACK 1\n  deliver 接收方 "0"\n  unmark RW[0..0]\n  slide RW to=1\n  deliver 接收方 "1"\n  unmark RW[1..1]\n  slide RW to=2\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 0"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 0、ACK 1；窗口滑到 2~4；发出 3、4\n  timer 发送方 "T0" stop\n  timer 发送方 "T1" stop\n  mark SW[0] done\n  mark SW[1] done\n  slide SW to=2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "3"\n  timer 发送方 "T3" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "4"\n  timer 发送方 "T4" start\nstep 接收方：缓存 3、4；回 ACK 3、ACK 4\n  buffer 接收方 "3"\n  mark RW[3] active\n  buffer 接收方 "4"\n  mark RW[4] active\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 3"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 4"\nstep 收到 ACK 3、ACK 4；帧 2 超时，只重发它\n  timer 发送方 "T3" stop\n  timer 发送方 "T4" stop\n  mark SW[3] done\n  mark SW[4] done\n  timer 发送方 "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\nstep 接收方：交付 2~4；回 ACK 2\n  deliver 接收方 "2 3 4"\n  unmark RW[2..4]\n  slide RW to=5\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2"\nstep 收到 ACK 2；窗口滑到 5~5；发出 5\n  timer 发送方 "T2" stop\n  mark SW[2] done\n  slide SW to=5\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "5"\n  timer 发送方 "T5" start\nstep 接收方：交付 5；回 ACK 5\n  deliver 接收方 "5"\n  unmark RW[5..5]\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 5"\nstep 收到 ACK 5；全部帧已确认\n  timer 发送方 "T5" stop\n  mark SW[5] done
title 对比
steps 10 scenes 3

tl 1 85.800
